# Spike 1431 transcript

Each heading gives the UTC time and the host the wrapper (`t.sh`) ran on. That host is always the laptop, `[laptop host]`. A command whose text starts `ssh … hostpc`, `gh auth token | ssh … hostpc` or `scp … hostpc:` runs on the Windows machine **Host-PC10** (`hostpc`); its output under the heading is Host-PC10's. The wrapper redacts any `gh?_` token string in the output. The token reached hostpc only on stdin.

Every command the spike ran, with its UTC time and host (`hostname -s`). Entries before the wrapper existed are backfilled from the session log with their recorded times.

### 2026-11-20T00:40:07Z `[laptop host]`

```
$ gh issue view 1431 -R williacj/rigger --json body -q .body | shasum | cut -c1-12; gh issue view 1431 -R williacj/rigger --json body -q .body | sed -n '/Hosts, and whose/,/Rules on how/p'
3c509245f0e7
**Hosts, and whose custody each is under.**

- **The WSL2 machine and the Windows machine are the primary hosts.** Heavy or long runs go there. The spike reaches each over SSH from the laptop ([relay](https://github.com/williacj/rigger/issues/1429#issuecomment-7014074437), answer 4), at these hosts:
  - the WSL2 machine: `wsl-hostpc` (`user@10.0.0.29`, port 2222, a Windows port proxy into WSL's sshd; until that sshd answers, WSL is reached through the Windows host with `ssh hostpc wsl.exe -e …`);
  - the Windows machine: `hostpc` (`user@10.0.0.29`, port 2223, Windows OpenSSH).
- **The laptop** (macOS) is enrolled under M8's custody rule ([M8-U45](https://github.com/williacj/rigger/issues/1293#issuecomment-6980322109), [M8-U58](https://github.com/williacj/rigger/issues/1293#issuecomment-6988027619)). It takes light macOS runs only, and none of these:
  - reading, writing or running anything in `~/GitHub/emend-engine`, `~/GitHub/emend-engine-worktrees`, `~/GitHub/emend` or `~/rigger-builds/`, on Emend's board (project 13), or in the williacj/emend repository;
  - a LaunchAgent with the `com.williacj.rigger` prefix, or anything in `~/Library/LaunchAgents/com.williacj.rigger*.plist` or `~/Library/Logs/rigger/`;
  - a Rigger run from a checkout, or one against Emend (`R-SAFE-5`);
  - a git GUI, or an IDE that fetches by itself;
  - a long CPU-heavy job during an M8 canary or window period, which rigger-ae announces.
- **The Mac mini** is under the M8 coordinator's custody and is offline. Before any use, the spike tells rigger-ae on this card and waits for rigger-ae to relay the M8 coordinator's OK. Every command there runs through `~/GitHub/rigger-worktrees/coordinator-tools/suite-slot.sh <tag> -- …`. The spike never touches the `judge-*` or `codex-*` clones there.

**Rules on how it runs.**
[exit 0]
```

```
$ cd ~/GitHub/rigger-worktrees/1431-spike-engineer && sed -n '380,402p' docs/v0-build-plan.md
- A missed trigger fires once on restart (`R-SCHED-8`).
- A card re-dispatched after an engine death is recorded as a redo, and `report` counts it apart
  from first-attempt work (D1) (`R-STATE-3`).
- `report` on this repository's own event stream shows the signals of every layer that has
  landed (`R-RECORD-2`, `R-RECORD-4`).
- Telemetry push comes from config: off by default writes nothing to the data ref, and on writes
  the stream there (`R-SAFE-3`).

**M8. Acceptance on Emend.**

In order:

1. Three concurrent cold `cargo build --workspace` dispatches in three real worktrees return 0,
   with survivors logged.
2. SIGKILL of Rigger during three live Engineers leaves no descendant within ten seconds, and the
   restart re-dispatches all three.
3. A bounded canary of ten Emend cards, including one requirements proposal judged by Emend's
   panel and then the owner. That panel needs two Emend assets that do not exist yet,
   a PM reviewer role and an architecture-review skill; both are Emend cards and both land first.
4. A thirty-card window with zero infrastructure-caused escalations, and every merge carrying a
   fresh verdict from every configured judge. Judged from `report`, not by hand.
5. Telemetry push is on for the whole window, and the pushed stream reads back complete
   afterwards. M9 and M10 have no other source of data.
[exit 0]
```
